2026-11-22 05:46:25 INFO     [autogluon.assistant.agents.executer_agent] Error summary: The provided code uses `AutoGluon.MultiModalPredictor` for a standard tabular regression task on the `auto_mpg` dataset. While `MultiModalPredictor` can handle tabular data, it is significantly heavier and less efficient than `TabularPredictor` for this specific use case. More importantly, the `__del__` method in the `AutoGluonRegressorWrapper` attempts to delete the model directory (`shutil.rmtree(self.path)`). In many environments, this causes race conditions or permission errors during the cross-validation loop if the predictor is still holding file handles or if the cleanup triggers prematurely. Additionally, the `MultiModalPredictor` is overkill for a simple regression task and may exceed time/resource constraints in a 10-fold CV setup compared to `TabularPredictor`.

While this is generally correct for AutoGluon, the `__del__` method attempts to remove this directory. In many environments, AutoGluon's background processes or file locks can prevent `shutil.rmtree` from successfully deleting the directory, leading to potential resource leaks or errors during cross-validation. Furthermore, the `fit` method is called with `time_limit=250` for each of the 10 folds. Given the overhead of initializing AutoGluon and the potential for file system contention, this may exceed the total execution time budget or cause instability. Additionally, the `fit` method does not explicitly handle the case where the model might already exist in the path, and the `__del__` cleanup is unreliable.
